```json
    "originalFile": "% conclusion.tex — compressed for 8-page limit\n\n\\section{Discussion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\n\\paragraph{Topic shortcut and label equivalence.}\nThree observations argue against a topic shortcut: unstable topic OOD (F1\\,=\\,0.541$\\pm$0.207), scrambled labels matching correct within 3.9\\,pp, and label-free MLM detection (F1\\,$\\geq$\\,0.97 at $K \\leq 5$). One hypothesis is that classification loss regularizes the representation space independently of label content, preventing catastrophic drift from the pretrained manifold; binary (2-class) and 9-class scrambled yield equivalent detection (F1 gap $\\leq$3.9\\,pp), spanning a 4.5$\\times$ class-count range consistent with the regularization interpretation; finer-grained class-count ablation could further distinguish these.\n\n\\paragraph{Single-LLM confound.}\nToxicChat (+18--42\\,pp; Table~\\ref{tab:human_data}) and cross-LLM experiments (Appendix~\\ref{app:cross_llm}) confirm transfer. Mixed-LLM 9-class maintains DD F1\\,=\\,0.818$\\pm$0.096 (+53\\,pp) while MLM collapses; vocabulary analysis confirms that benign texts from different LLMs diverge primarily in topic distribution (KL\\,$>$\\,1\\,bit) rather than stylistic markers, so MLM predictably acquires source-specific topic patterns while classification supervision, operating on strategy labels independent of surface form, remains robust. Cross-LLM error decomposition confirms F1 degradation is driven by false positives on unfamiliar benign text, not missed attacks.\n\n\\paragraph{Variance reversal on human data.}\nClassification supervision is associated with lower variance on synthetic OOD (4--20$\\times$ observed range, 3 seeds); on ToxicChat this reverses (std\\,=\\,0.087 vs.\\ 0.013). Frozen probing localizes MLM instability to Stage-2 (Appendix~\\ref{app:frozen_verify}).\n\n\\paragraph{Encoder capacity, temporal, and lexical analysis.}\nThree encoders (125M--355M) differ in pretraining; same-architecture scaling is needed. Mean pooling matches BiGRU ($|\\Delta| < 1$\\,pp), confirming order-invariant detection. TF-IDF collapses on ActorAttack (0.261 vs.\\ 0.961 on DD).\n\n\\paragraph{Benign distribution as the remaining bottleneck.}\nOOD F1 figures are upper bounds (benign is IID). On WildChat, 9-class FPR reaches 68.1\\% (Appendix~\\ref{app:wildchat_fpr}); cross-LLM error decomposition confirms collapse is driven by false positives on unfamiliar benign text.\n\n\\section{Conclusion}\n\nOn synthetic multi-turn evaluations, classification-based DAPT is the most robust adaptation for base-size encoders; unsupervised pretraining collapses under mixed-LLM training. Negative results on label correctness (TOST-confirmed on DD at $n$\\,=\\,5; $d$\\,=\\,0.18 on ActorAttack), temporal order, and domain specificity constrain which properties matter; continued pretraining is 266$\\times$ faster than LLM-as-judge (Appendix~\\ref{app:efficiency}), offering a practical alternative given matched benign distributions (Appendix~\\ref{app:wildchat_fpr}).\n\n\\section{Limitations}\n\\label{sec:limitations}\n\n\\paragraph{Multi-turn human validation.}\nAll multi-turn findings are derived from synthetic conversations generated by Qwen3-8B. ToxicChat validates transfer to human-authored single-turn attacks (+18--42\\,pp over vanilla), but no human-generated multi-turn jailbreak dataset exists for evaluation. Whether the evidence hierarchy and classification supervision advantages generalize to human-authored multi-turn attacks remains the primary open question.\n\n\\paragraph{Turn-count distribution.}\nBenign conversations contain 12--20 turns, DD jailbreaks 6--10, and ActorAttack jailbreaks 3--8; the three categories have non-overlapping turn counts. Three observations argue against length as the primary detection cue: (a) early detection at equal-length prefixes $K$=1 and $K$=2 achieves AA OOD F1\\,=\\,0.773 and 0.935 (Appendix~\\ref{tab:actorattack_full}); (b) vanilla models access the same length signal but achieve near-zero OOD recall; and (c) truncating benign conversations to 3 turns yields FPR\\,=\\,0\\% (Appendix~\\ref{app:length_independence}).\n\n\\paragraph{Temporal aggregation dilution.}\nWhen subtle attacks (ActorAttack) are followed by extended benign padding, BiGRU temporal aggregation dilutes attack signals (F1 drops from 0.963 to 0.383; Appendix~\\ref{app:length_independence}); in practice, adversaries would not dilute their own successful attacks.\n\n\\paragraph{OOD benign distribution.}\nOOD results reflect attack-side generalization only (Section~\\ref{sec:discussion}). Even with matched benign distributions, residual calibration error (ECE\\,=\\,0.126--0.141; Appendix~\\ref{app:calibration}) suggests temperature scaling is needed for deployment.\n\n\\paragraph{Frozen encoder assumption.}\nThe frozen Stage-1 design isolates representation quality but may underestimate full-pipeline performance. End-to-end fine-tuning closes this gap to $\\leq$5.8\\,pp on ActorAttack (F1\\,=\\,0.997$\\pm$0.005 vs.\\ 0.939$\\pm$0.047 frozen; Table~\\ref{tab:e2e}; Appendix~\\ref{app:frozen_verify}).\n\n\\paragraph{Dataset scale and cross-LLM transfer.}\nScale validation (1{,}000 conversations; Appendix~\\ref{app:scale_validation}) confirms the hierarchy holds (DAPT advantage 40--56\\,pp over vanilla). Cross-LLM validation shows jailbreak detection transfers when benign distribution is held constant (F1 recovers to 0.800--1.000), but benign-side F1 drops to 0.370--0.430 with unfamiliar benign text (Appendix~\\ref{app:cross_llm}). The perturbation evaluation ($n$=76) remains underpowered.\n\n\\paragraph{Strategy labels.}\nPersuasion strategy labels ($n$=50 human validation, $\\kappa$=0.78) are produced by Qwen3-8B; the validation sample yields small per-class sizes across 9 categories, limiting reliability estimates for individual strategies. OOD strategy accuracy degrades to 30.7\\% ($\\kappa$=0.126); interpretability is limited to known attack families. We plan to release annotation prompts and data for independent verification.\n\n\\paragraph{Detection architecture.}\nBinary vs.\\ 9-class equivalence (F1 gap $\\leq$3.9\\,pp) suggests the binary head is not the critical differentiator. FITD provides minimal discriminative signal (all variants F1\\,$\\geq$\\,0.990), serving as confirmation that simple attacks do not challenge any variant.\n\n\\paragraph{Encoder scale.}\nWhether findings hold at LLM-scale encoders (7B+) remains open; same-architecture scaling (e.g., DeBERTa-base vs.\\ DeBERTa-large) is needed to attribute the capacity effect to scale alone.\n\n\\paragraph{Scope and single-turn transfer.}\nOnly persuasion-mediated multi-turn attacks fall within scope; single-turn token-level attacks~\\citep{zou2023gcg}, non-persuasive strategies, and non-text modalities are not addressed. ToxicChat performance reflects zero-shot single-turn transfer; doubling the training corpus drops ToxicChat macro F1 from 0.596 to 0.341$\\pm$0.004 while multi-turn OOD remains stable, confirming the architecture targets multi-turn detection.\n\n\\paragraph{Computation cost.}\nTotal pipeline cost for all seven variants is under 4 GPU-hours on a single A100.\n\n\\section*{Ethics Statement}\n\nThis work is defensive: it improves detection of multi-turn jailbreak attacks rather than developing new attacks. We acknowledge dual-use risk, as the persuasion taxonomy could inform adversarial actors. We mitigate this by using only synthetic conversations (no real harmful content), withholding attack-generation prompts, and focusing contributions on detection mechanisms. No human subjects were involved; all data will be released under CC BY-NC 4.0.\n",
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01NCUZXXHsmwFkDKrcKoCYcC`</summary>

```text
112	All three classification-supervised variants converge to equivalent detection (F1 = 0.994--1.000); McNemar's ($p > 0.13$) and paired permutation tests ($p \geq 0.40$) provide descriptive evidence suggesting equivalence. Extended to $n$=5 seeds, TOST (margin = 5\,pp) confirms equivalence on DD ($p < 0.001$; Table~\ref{tab:tost_sensitivity}); on ActorAttack, the mean gap narrows to 0.5\,pp with TOST approaching significance ($p$\,=\,0.08, SE\,=\,0.026). Cohen's $d$ for 9-class vs.\ scrambled is negligible on both DD ($d \approx 0$) and ActorAttack ($d$\,=\,0.09 at $n$=5, down from 0.59 at $n$=3; Table~\ref{tab:seed_level}). Label quality and granularity are non-factors for detection.
```

```json
{
  "type": "queue-operation",

  "timestamp": "2026-05-21T10:09:03.278Z",
  "sessionId": "6629599a-6895-4670-a62b-6052633f0dc0",
  "content": "[审稿报告]\nOverall: 3.0 - Findings\n\n# 审稿报告 — DisPeD: A Controlled Study of Encoder Adaptation for Multi-Turn Jailbreak Detection\n\n**目标 venue**: EMNLP 2026 via ARR May 2026 cycle\n**Deadline**: 2026-05-25 (AoE)\n**审稿模式**: Fresh (重新审稿)\n**Reviewer 编排**: 2 人（R1: 方法严谨性+实验设计+统计检验; R2: 新颖性+完整性+Related Work+格式合规）\n**轮次**: Round 1 → 交叉分享 → 共识达成（1 轮收敛）\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n本文提出 DisPeD，一个两阶段多轮越狱检测流水线：Stage 1 通过域自适应预训练（DAPT）在 DeBERTa-v3 上训练 7 种变体（9-class/binary/scrambled/MLM-only/topic/Wiki-MLM/vanilla），Stage 2 用冻结的 BiGRU 聚合 [CLS] 嵌入进行对话级二元分类。通过 13 种编码器配置（7 变体 × 多种规模）的控制消融，论文分解了域暴露、分类监督、标签质量和粒度对跨攻击泛化的贡献。核心发现：(1) 域暴露是跨攻击迁移的主要机制（MLM 关闭 ~60pp 的 OOD gap）；(2) 分类监督提供稳定性但标签正确性/粒度不重要（scrambled ≈ 9-class, TOST-confirmed on DD at n=5）；(3) 时序建模非必需（mean pooling ≈ BiGRU）。实验在合成多轮数据（Qwen3-8B 生成，4 个攻击家族）上进行，辅以 ToxicChat 人类单轮验证和 WildChat FPR 评估。\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n**S1. [共识] 消融设计系统性强。** 7 种 Stage-1 变体的 factorial design 在 NLP 安全领域罕见，每个变体在概念上隔离一个因素。冻结 Stage-1 encoder 确保消融公平性。这种分解思路比\"方法 A vs B\"对比更有说服力。[R1 S1, R2 S1]\n\n**S2. [共识] Scrambled ≈ 9-class 发现有独立学术价值。** 随机打乱标签产生等效检测性能（DD TOST p<0.001 at n=5; AA Cohen's d=0.09 negligible），说明分类监督以任务无关的方式改善表示质量，对域自适应的更广泛理解有启发意义。[R1 S2, R2 S1]\n\n**S3. [共识] 多维度互补证据。** CKA 热力图、t-SNE 嵌入可视化、策略转移矩阵、前缀级分析、扰动鲁棒性、跨编码器验证、跨 LLM 验证、数据效率分析——提供了 mechanistic insight 而非单纯指标堆叠。[R1 S3, R2 S3]\n\n**S4. [共识] 诚实面对负面结果。** Discussion 坦承 MLM 在 mixed-LLM 下崩溃、ToxicChat 上证据层级逆转、WildChat FPR 过高等问题。Limitations 覆盖广泛（10 个段落）。[R1 S4]\n\n**S5. [共识] 可复现性完备。** 超参数完整列出，计算预算清晰（单卡 A100 共 4 GPU-hours），代码/数据计划以 MIT / CC BY-NC 4.0 发布。Appendix 详尽（22 页补充材料）。[R1 S5, R2 S5]\n\n**S6. [共识] 写作质量好。** 行文简洁，无明显 AI 生成痕迹，符号使用基本一致，Figure 1 pipeline 图清晰。[R2 S4]\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）\n\n**MF1. [共识] 正文超过 8 页限制——可能触发 desk reject。** Section 6 (Conclusion) 起始于第 9 页顶部，主文（Sections 1-6）约 8.1-8.2 页。EMNLP 2026 严格执行 8 页限制（Limitations 和 Ethics 不计入）。视觉审查 Agent 独立确认此问题。**注意：此问题在 Round 3 曾被标记为已解决，当前为 regression。** [R2 W1, R1 M1, Visual Check]\n\n**MF2. [共识] 缺少与任何已发表多轮防御系统的直接实验对比。** 论文引用了 BIID、G-Guard、SafeDream、X-Boundary、JBShield 等 5+ 个已发表系统，但实验中未运行任何一个。Vanilla DeBERTa+BiGRU 声称 \"equivalent to DeepContext\" 但此等价性未经验证。LLM-as-judge baseline 也存在公平性问题（未经 domain-specific 训练，GPT-5.5 30-47% 不可解析可能是 prompt 设计问题）。没有外部校准点，性能声明缺乏参照系。**历史 issue R1-N2-v2，跨 6 轮未解决。** [R1 N1, R2 W7]\n\n**MF3. [共识] OOD 评估中 benign 是 IID，系统性膨胀了 OOD F1。** OOD 评估集的攻击来自未见家族但 benign 与训练同分布。OOD F1 衡量的是\"在熟悉良性背景下检测新攻击\"——这是显著更简单的任务。Table 2 中 F1 ≥ 0.99 为 upper bound。WildChat FPR=44-68% 从反面验证了此问题。**历史 issue R5-N1，跨 4 轮未解决。** [R1 N2, R2 W3]\n\n**MF4. [共识] TOST 等价性检验统计效力不足。** n=3 时 TOST 功效 < 0.3。即使扩展到 n=5：DD confirmed (p<0.001) 但 AA 仍 p=0.08 formally inconclusive。5pp margin 无理论依据（安全关键场景中 5pp 可能是实质性差异）。Bootstrap CI 跨种子池化（76→228）违反独立性假设。\"scrambled ≈ 9-class\" 是核心发现之一，统计基础需加固。**历史 issue R6-N2，部分缓解但未完全解决。** [R1 N4]\n\n**MF5. [共识] 新颖性有限——核心发现的\"反直觉\"程度被高估。** DAPT 有效性是 Gururangan et al. (2020) 已知结论；\"标签正确性不重要\"在 SSL 文献（SimCLR/BYOL）中已有先例；\"时序建模不重要\"在 bag-of-features 文献中已被观察到；\"粒度不重要\"在 transfer learning 中是常见现象。增量贡献是将这些已知现象在 jailbreak detection 领域系统验证——这有价值但不足以支撑 main conference 的 novelty 要求。**历史 issue R2-N1-v2，跨 7 轮持续存在。** [R2 W2]\n\n**MF6. [共识] WildChat FPR 44-68% 严重削弱实用价值主张。** Introduction 中 \"offering a practical alternative given matched benign distributions\" 暗示实用价值，但真实数据上将近一半到三分之二的正常对话被误判。ROC AUC=0.700 表明表征层面区分力不足，post-hoc calibration 无法解决。如果速度优势的前提是系统可用，那么在 FPR 如此高时速度优势无意义。**历史 issue R6-W1。** [R2 W3, R1 context]\n\n### Should-Fix（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**SF1. [共识] 数据规模和合成依赖。** 训练 350 条合成对话（Qwen3-8B 单一来源）、ID 测试 76 条、OOD 118 条。仅 1 个有效 OOD 攻击家族（ActorAttack，因 FITD 无区分度、DD 被 TF-IDF 高度解决）。同一模型既生成数据又做标注引入系统性偏差。ToxicChat 是单轮，无法验证多轮 claim。**历史 issue R4-N3。** [R1 N3, R2 W5]\n\n**SF2. [共识] \"Single-factor isolation\" 声明过度。** Binary vs 9-class 同时改变粒度和分类目标数量；Topic vs 9-class 同时改变语义内容和类别数（5 vs 9）；MLM-only 同时移除分类监督和改变训练目标。论文应更精确描述每个对比实际隔离了什么。[R1 W3]\n\n**SF3. [共识] Scrambled ≈ 9-class 削弱 taxonomy 贡献的叙事张力。** 如果打乱标签产生等效检测，9 类 taxonomy 在检测上无因果作用。论文试图同时声称 taxonomy 为贡献又承认标签不重要——叙事逻辑矛盾。此外 n=50 人工验证对 9-class 偏小（每类仅 ~5.5 样本），且如果标签不重要，为何投入 effort 验证标签质量？**历史 issue R6-W2 + R1-W4-v2。** [R1 W4, R2 W8]\n\n**SF4. Related Work 遗漏 domain-specific pretraining 前辈。** BioBERT (Lee et al., 2020) 和 SciBERT (Beltagy et al., 2019) 是 continued pretraining on domain text for encoder-based classifiers 的代表性前辈工作，直接相关但未被讨论。在讨论 label correctness 时，SSL 文献（SimCLR/BYOL）应作为更广泛 context 被引用。[R2 W6]\n\n**SF5. FITD 无区分度削弱\"4 攻击家族\"声称。** 所有变体在 FITD 上 F1≈1.000，对消融无贡献。有效鉴别力仅来自 ActorAttack（DD 被 TF-IDF 高度解决）。论文应坦承这一点。[R2 W4]\n\n**SF6. Population std (n divisor) 低估变异性。** n=3 时 population std 比 Bessel-corrected (n-1) 低约 18%，使误差条看起来人为偏紧。虽论文声称不影响结论，但报告样本统计量时这是非标准做法。[R1 W5]\n\n**SF7. 术语不一致。** \"cross-attack generalization\" 和 \"OOD\" 交替使用，但本文仅攻击侧分布漂移（benign 是 IID）。K 有时表示总轮数有时表示截断位置。**历史 issue R2-TERM。** [R1 M3, R2 W9]\n\n### Minor（不影响接收）\n\n- Figure 2 柱状图灰度打印下颜色难以区分，建议添加 hatching pattern [R2 W10, R1 M2]\n- Figure 1 内部文字标注字号偏小，接近可读性下限 [Visual Check]\n- Pages 6-8 连续 3 页 \"顶部 table + 下方纯文字\" 视觉节奏单调 [Visual Check]\n- TF-IDF 在 K=2 时 F1=1.000 暗示短对话存在平凡词汇线索，值得讨论 [R1 M4]\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Consensus [AGREE]\n\n两位 Reviewer 在以下关键问题上完全一致：\n\n1. **Overall 评分 3.0 (Findings)**——执行扎实但新颖性和实用性不足以支撑 main conference\n2. **页数超限**是格式硬伤，可能触发 desk reject\n3. **IID benign 膨胀 OOD F1** 是系统性评估缺陷\n4. **WildChat FPR 44-68%** 严重限制实用价值\n5. **缺少已发表 baseline** 使性能声明缺乏参照系\n6. **消融设计** 在概念层面优秀，是论文最大亮点\n7. **Scrambled ≈ 9-class** 有学术价值但统计基础需加固\n8. **Appendix 详尽**，可复现性好\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Divergence [REBUT]\n\n**分歧点 1: End-to-end fine-tuning 的必要性**\n- R1: Frozen encoder 可能低估 vanilla 潜力，需要 E2E 对照 [Should-Fix]\n- R2: Frozen encoder 是方法论优势而非缺陷，Appendix N 已提供 E2E 结果（Δ≤2.5pp）[REBUT]\n- **Director 判断**: R2 的反驳有据——E2E 数据已存在且差距极小。降级为 info。\n\n**分歧点 2: Missing references 的严重性**\n- R2: BioBERT/SciBERT/CoSafe/SimCLR 等为 Must-Fix 级别遗漏\n- R1: 未发现关键缺失引用，现有覆盖全面\n- **Director 判断**: BioBERT/SciBERT 确实与 DAPT 方法论直接相关，但非致命遗漏。列为 Should-Fix。\n\n---\n\n## Questions（需作者澄清）\n\n1. 如果在 OOD 评估中加入不同领域的 benign 对话（如客服、学术问答），F1 ≥ 0.99 是否还能维持？[R1 Q1]\n2. TOST 等价性被拒绝的最小 margin 是多少？能否提供 n=3 和 n=5 的 power analysis？[R1 Q2, Q6]\n3. Scrambled labels 中 random permutation 的种子是否会影响结果？不同 permutation 的 variance 有多大？[R2 Q1]\n4. 为什么没有在同一数据上运行已发表的多轮防御系统？是数据格式不兼容、代码不可用，还是方法论选择？[R1 Q4]\n5. Mixed-LLM 如果加入更多样化的 LLM，能否改善 WildChat FPR？[R2 Q2]\n6. RoBERTa-base vanilla 已达 DD 0.981——DAPT 的价值是否 architecture-dependent？[R2 Q3]\n7. 放松 BERTScore 约束（如 0.85 或 0.80）时，detection 是否显著下降？[R2 Q5]\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n**R2 提出（R1 未发现关键缺失）：**\n- BioBERT (Lee et al., 2020) — domain-specific continued pretraining 前辈\n- SciBERT (Beltagy et al., 2019) — 同上\n- CoSafe (Yu et al., 2024) — 多轮对话安全评估基准\n- SimCLR / BYOL / MoCo — label correctness debate 的更广泛 context\n- WildJailbreak (Jiang et al., 2024) — in-the-wild jailbreak 数据集\n\n---\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n外部审稿未配置（`external_review.paperreview_ai.enabled` 未设置）。无外部审稿结果。\n\n---\n\n## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）\n\n视觉审查 Agent 已完成（2026-05-21 09:51 → 09:56，耗时约 5 分钟）。\n\n**Warning (2)**:\n1. **Page 9**: 正文 Section 6 (Conclusion) 溢出至第 9 页——与 R2 W1 / R1 M1 一致\n2. **Pages 6-8**: 连续 3 页 \"顶部 table + 下方纯文字\" 的单调视觉节奏\n\n**Info (10)**:\n- Page 1: 首页无视觉锚点（纯文字），Figure 1 在 page 2 出现\n- Page 2: Figure 1 内部文字标注字号偏小\n- Page 5: Figure 2 色彩区分度良好，色盲友好\n- Page 5: Table 2 booktabs 风格规范，±std 格式统一\n- Page 9: Limitations 分段清晰，符合 EMNLP 要求\n- Pages 13-14: Appendix 大型表格跨页呈现正常\n- Page 15: Figure 3（heatmaps）双栏内尺寸偏小\n- Page 16: Figures 4+5 与 Tables 密度高但可读\n- Pages 10-11: References 格式统一，无 [?] 未解析引用\n- Page 22: Responsible NLP Research Checklist 完整填写\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n### 评分\n\n| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 综合 |\n|------|-----|-----|------|\n| Soundness | 3/5 | 3.5/5 | 3/5 |\n| Excitement | 3/5 | 2.5/5 | 2.5/5 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 4/5 | 4/5 |\n| **Overall** | **3.0** | **3.0** | **3.0** |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 4/5 | — |\n\n**Overall: 3.0 — Findings（可被 Findings 接收）**\n\n### 总评\n\n一项执行扎实的控制消融研究，系统地分解了 encoder adaptation 在多轮 jailbreak 检测中的各因素贡献。消融设计是最大亮点——7 种变体的 factorial approach 在 NLP 安全领域少见。Domain-exposure > label-correctness 的发现有真正的学术价值，scrambled ≈ correct labels 的观察对 DAPT 理论有启发意义。\n\n但论文面临三个结构性问题：(1) 核心发现在 DAPT/SSL 文献中均有先例，增量新颖性有限——更适合定位为\"安全关键领域的系统验证\"而非\"反直觉发现\"；(2) IID benign 膨胀了 OOD F1，WildChat FPR 44-68% 暴露了真实场景下的严重局限，削弱了论文的实用价值叙事；(3) 关键统计检验（TOST n=3/5、跨种子池化 bootstrap CI）的效力不足以支撑核心结论的确定性程度。\n\n此外，正文超过 8 页限制和缺少已发表 baseline 的实验对比是需要立即解决的格式/实验问题。\n\n**建议接收为 Findings**，条件是修复页数超限、降级统计声明、更诚实地定位新颖性和实用价值。\n\n---\n\n## Detailed Comments\n\n### 按章节详细意见\n\n**Abstract**: 数值使用克制（~5 个），scope 限定为 \"synthetic multi-turn data\" 是正确的。但 \"condition-dependent\" 和 \"evidence hierarchy\" 首次出现时缺乏直观解释。\n\n**Introduction**: Contribution 列表结构清晰但信息密度仍偏高。\"Counter-intuitive findings\" 的 framing 需降温——在更广泛 ML 文献中这些并非反直觉。第四段结果预览偏长（历史 issue R2-W4-v2 未解决）。\n\n**Related Work**: 覆盖面广（31 篇引用），但遗漏 domain-specific BERT 前辈（BioBERT/SciBERT）。与 computational persuasion 文献的联系充分。\n\n**Method (Section 3)**: Table 1 清晰传达 7 种变体设计。但 \"single-factor isolation\" 声明需加 caveat（Binary/Topic/MLM 存在混淆）。Strategy annotation 质量验证不足（n=50, κ=0.78）。\n\n**Experiments (Section 4)**:\n- **Setup**: 数据规模偏小（350 训练，76/118 测试）。OOD benign = IID 是系统性缺陷。\n- **Cross-Attack (4.2)**: Table 2 清晰但 F1 ≥ 0.99 应标注为 upper bound。\n- **Decomposition (4.3)**: Evidence hierarchy 是论文核心贡献。TOST 统计基础已改善（n=5）但 AA 仍 inconclusive。4-20× variance reduction 需降级为描述性观察。\n- **Cross-Encoder (4.4)**: RoBERTa-large vanilla 1.000 on AA 是重要发现，但 scale claim 被架构差异混淆（历史 issue R1-CAPACITY）。\n- **Mixed-LLM (4.5)**: MLM 崩溃是重要负面结果。\n- **Human Data (4.6)**: ToxicChat 是单轮——正文应更明确标注。\n- **Perturbation (4.7)**: AA 下 DAPT 降 20pp 是重要局限。\n- **Strategy (4.8)**: OOD κ=0.126 坦诚但削弱 taxonomy 价值。\n\n**Discussion (Section 5)**: 与 Introduction contributions 有重复。\"Benign distribution as the remaining bottleneck\" 段落重要但应提前至更显眼位置。\n\n**Conclusion (Section 6)**: 溢出到第 9 页——必须压缩。\n\n**Limitations (Section 7)**: 覆盖广泛（10 段），内容充实。但正文 framing 与 Limitations 中承认的局限之间存在温度差。\n\n**Appendix**: 极其详尽（22 页），包含 per-prefix 分析、CKA、cross-LLM、TOST sensitivity、数据效率等。Reproducibility details 完备。\n\n### 历史 issue 追踪摘要\n\n本轮（Round 8 fresh review）重新发现了以下长期未解决的 issues：\n- **R2-N1-v2** (新颖性不足): 跨 7 轮持续存在 → 本轮再次确认\n- **R1-N2-v2** (缺少已发表 baseline): 跨 6 轮未解决 → 本轮再次确认\n- **R5-N1** (IID benign): 跨 4 轮未解决 → 本轮再次确认\n- **R6-N2** (TOST 效力): 部分缓解（n=5）但未完全解决 → 本轮再次确认\n- **R2-W6-v2** (页数超限): Round 3 标记为已解决，**当前为 regression**\n\n新发现 issues：\n- **R8-N1**: Single-factor isolation 声明过度\n- **R8-N2**: FITD 无区分度\n- **R8-N3**: BioBERT/SciBERT 等 related work 遗漏\n- **R8-N4**: LLM baseline 对比公平性\n---\n\n## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)\n\n**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:\n\n1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),\n   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。\n\n2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:\n   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长\n   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补\n   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本\n   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据\n   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)\n\n3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:\n   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有\n   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的\n   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。\n\n4. **收到 Reviewer [审稿报告]
```
Overall: 3.0 - Findings

# 审稿报告 — DisPeD: A Controlled Study of Encoder Adaptation for Multi-Turn Jailbreak Detection

**目标 venue**: EMNLP 2026 via ARR May 2026 cycle
**Deadline**: 2026-05-25 (AoE)
**审稿模式**: Fresh (重新审稿)
**Reviewer 编排**: 2 人（R1: 方法严谨性+实验设计+统计检验; R2: 新颖性+完整性+Related Work+格式合规）
**轮次**: Round 1 → 交叉分享 → 共识达成（1 轮收敛）

---

## Summary

本文提出 DisPeD，一个两阶段多轮越狱检测流水线：Stage 1 通过域自适应预训练（DAPT）在 DeBERTa-v3 上训练 7 种变体（9-class/binary/scrambled/MLM-only/topic/Wiki-MLM/vanilla），Stage 2 用冻结的 BiGRU 聚合 [CLS] 嵌入进行对话级二元分类。通过 13 种编码器配置（7 变体 × 多种规模）的控制消融，论文分解了域暴露、分类监督、标签质量和粒度对跨攻击泛化的贡献。核心发现：(1) 域暴露是跨攻击迁移的主要机制（MLM 关闭 ~60pp 的 OOD gap）；(2) 分类监督提供稳定性但标签正确性/粒度不重要（scrambled ≈ 9-class, TOST-confirmed on DD at n=5）；(3) 时序建模非必需（mean pooling ≈ BiGRU）。实验在合成多轮数据（Qwen3-8B 生成，4 个攻击家族）上进行，辅以 ToxicChat 人类单轮验证和 WildChat FPR 评估。

---

## Strengths

**S1. [共识] 消融设计系统性强。** 7 种 Stage-1 变体的 factorial design 在 NLP 安全领域罕见，每个变体在概念上隔离一个因素。冻结 Stage-1 encoder 确保消融公平性。这种分解思路比"方法 A vs B"对比更有说服力。[R1 S1, R2 S1]

**S2. [共识] Scrambled ≈ 9-class 发现有独立学术价值。** 随机打乱标签产生等效检测性能（DD TOST p<0.001 at n=5; AA Cohen's d=0.09 negligible），说明分类监督以任务无关的方式改善表示质量，对域自适应的更广泛理解有启发意义。[R1 S2, R2 S1]

**S3. [共识] 多维度互补证据。** CKA 热力图、t-SNE 嵌入可视化、策略转移矩阵、前缀级分析、扰动鲁棒性、跨编码器验证、跨 LLM 验证、数据效率分析——提供了 mechanistic insight 而非单纯指标堆叠。[R1 S3, R2 S3]

**S4. [共识] 诚实面对负面结果。** Discussion 坦承 MLM 在 mixed-LLM 下崩溃、ToxicChat 上证据层级逆转、WildChat FPR 过高等问题。Limitations 覆盖广泛（10 个段落）。[R1 S4]

**S5. [共识] 可复现性完备。** 超参数完整列出，计算预算清晰（单卡 A100 共 4 GPU-hours），代码/数据计划以 MIT / CC BY-NC 4.0 发布。Appendix 详尽（22 页补充材料）。[R1 S5, R2 S5]

**S6. [共识] 写作质量好。** 行文简洁，无明显 AI 生成痕迹，符号使用基本一致，Figure 1 pipeline 图清晰。[R2 S4]

---

## Weaknesses

### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）

**MF1. [共识] 正文超过 8 页限制——可能触发 desk reject。** Section 6 (Conclusion) 起始于第 9 页顶部，主文（Sections 1-6）约 8.1-8.2 页。EMNLP 2026 严格执行 8 页限制（Limitations 和 Ethics 不计入）。视觉审查 Agent 独立确认此问题。**注意：此问题在 Round 3 曾被标记为已解决，当前为 regression。** [R2 W1, R1 M1, Visual Check]

**MF2. [共识] 缺少与任何已发表多轮防御系统的直接实验对比。** 论文引用了 BIID、G-Guard、SafeDream、X-Boundary、JBShield 等 5+ 个已发表系统，但实验中未运行任何一个。Vanilla DeBERTa+BiGRU 声称 "equivalent to DeepContext" 但此等价性未经验证。LLM-as-judge baseline 也存在公平性问题（未经 domain-specific 训练，GPT-5.5 30-47% 不可解析可能是 prompt 设计问题）。没有外部校准点，性能声明缺乏参照系。**历史 issue R1-N2-v2，跨 6 轮未解决。** [R1 N1, R2 W7]

**MF3. [共识] OOD 评估中 benign 是 IID，系统性膨胀了 OOD F1。** OOD 评估集的攻击来自未见家族但 benign 与训练同分布。OOD F1 衡量的是"在熟悉良性背景下检测新攻击"——这是显著更简单的任务。Table 2 中 F1 ≥ 0.99 为 upper bound。WildChat FPR=44-68% 从反面验证了此问题。**历史 issue R5-N1，跨 4 轮未解决。** [R1 N2, R2 W3]

**MF4. [共识] TOST 等价性检验统计效力不足。** n=3 时 TOST 功效 < 0.3。即使扩展到 n=5：DD confirmed (p<0.001) 但 AA 仍 p=0.08 formally inconclusive。5pp margin 无理论依据（安全关键场景中 5pp 可能是实质性差异）。Bootstrap CI 跨种子池化（76→228）违反独立性假设。"scrambled ≈ 9-class" 是核心发现之一，统计基础需加固。**历史 issue R6-N2，部分缓解但未完全解决。** [R1 N4]

**MF5. [共识] 新颖性有限——核心发现的"反直觉"程度被高估。** DAPT 有效性是 Gururangan et al. (2020) 已知结论；"标签正确性不重要"在 SSL 文献（SimCLR/BYOL）中已有先例；"时序建模不重要"在 bag-of-features 文献中已被观察到；"粒度不重要"在 transfer learning 中是常见现象。增量贡献是将这些已知现象在 jailbreak detection 领域系统验证——这有价值但不足以支撑 main conference 的 novelty 要求。**历史 issue R2-N1-v2，跨 7 轮持续存在。** [R2 W2]

**MF6. [共识] WildChat FPR 44-68% 严重削弱实用价值主张。** Introduction 中 "offering a practical alternative given matched benign distributions" 暗示实用价值，但真实数据上将近一半到三分之二的正常对话被误判。ROC AUC=0.700 表明表征层面区分力不足，post-hoc calibration 无法解决。如果速度优势的前提是系统可用，那么在 FPR 如此高时速度优势无意义。**历史 issue R6-W1。** [R2 W3, R1 context]

### Should-Fix（影响评分但不一票否决）

**SF1. [共识] 数据规模和合成依赖。** 训练 350 条合成对话（Qwen3-8B 单一来源）、ID 测试 76 条、OOD 118 条。仅 1 个有效 OOD 攻击家族（ActorAttack，因 FITD 无区分度、DD 被 TF-IDF 高度解决）。同一模型既生成数据又做标注引入系统性偏差。ToxicChat 是单轮，无法验证多轮 claim。**历史 issue R4-N3。** [R1 N3, R2 W5]

**SF2. [共识] "Single-factor isolation" 声明过度。** Binary vs 9-class 同时改变粒度和分类目标数量；Topic vs 9-class 同时改变语义内容和类别数（5 vs 9）；MLM-only 同时移除分类监督和改变训练目标。论文应更精确描述每个对比实际隔离了什么。[R1 W3]

**SF3. [共识] Scrambled ≈ 9-class 削弱 taxonomy 贡献的叙事张力。** 如果打乱标签产生等效检测，9 类 taxonomy 在检测上无因果作用。论文试图同时声称 taxonomy 为贡献又承认标签不重要——叙事逻辑矛盾。此外 n=50 人工验证对 9-class 偏小（每类仅 ~5.5 样本），且如果标签不重要，为何投入 effort 验证标签质量？**历史 issue R6-W2 + R1-W4-v2。** [R1 W4, R2 W8]

**SF4. Related Work 遗漏 domain-specific pretraining 前辈。** BioBERT (Lee et al., 2020) 和 SciBERT (Beltagy et al., 2019) 是 continued pretraining on domain text for encoder-based classifiers 的代表性前辈工作，直接相关但未被讨论。在讨论 label correctness 时，SSL 文献（SimCLR/BYOL）应作为更广泛 context 被引用。[R2 W6]

**SF5. FITD 无区分度削弱"4 攻击家族"声称。** 所有变体在 FITD 上 F1≈1.000，对消融无贡献。有效鉴别力仅来自 ActorAttack（DD 被 TF-IDF 高度解决）。论文应坦承这一点。[R2 W4]

**SF6. Population std (n divisor) 低估变异性。** n=3 时 population std 比 Bessel-corrected (n-1) 低约 18%，使误差条看起来人为偏紧。虽论文声称不影响结论，但报告样本统计量时这是非标准做法。[R1 W5]

**SF7. 术语不一致。** "cross-attack generalization" 和 "OOD" 交替使用，但本文仅攻击侧分布漂移（benign 是 IID）。K 有时表示总轮数有时表示截断位置。**历史 issue R2-TERM。** [R1 M3, R2 W9]

### Minor（不影响接收）

- Figure 2 柱状图灰度打印下颜色难以区分，建议添加 hatching pattern [R2 W10, R1 M2]
- Figure 1 内部文字标注字号偏小，接近可读性下限 [Visual Check]
- Pages 6-8 连续 3 页 "顶部 table + 下方纯文字" 视觉节奏单调 [Visual Check]
- TF-IDF 在 K=2 时 F1=1.000 暗示短对话存在平凡词汇线索，值得讨论 [R1 M4]

---

## Reviewer Consensus [AGREE]

两位 Reviewer 在以下关键问题上完全一致：

1. **Overall 评分 3.0 (Findings)**——执行扎实但新颖性和实用性不足以支撑 main conference
2. **页数超限**是格式硬伤，可能触发 desk reject
3. **IID benign 膨胀 OOD F1** 是系统性评估缺陷
4. **WildChat FPR 44-68%** 严重限制实用价值
5. **缺少已发表 baseline** 使性能声明缺乏参照系
6. **消融设计** 在概念层面优秀，是论文最大亮点
7. **Scrambled ≈ 9-class** 有学术价值但统计基础需加固
8. **Appendix 详尽**，可复现性好

---

## Reviewer Divergence [REBUT]

**分歧点 1: End-to-end fine-tuning 的必要性**
- R1: Frozen encoder 可能低估 vanilla 潜力，需要 E2E 对照 [Should-Fix]
- R2: Frozen encoder 是方法论优势而非缺陷，Appendix N 已提供 E2E 结果（Δ≤2.5pp）[REBUT]
- **Director 判断**: R2 的反驳有据——E2E 数据已存在且差距极小。降级为 info。

**分歧点 2: Missing references 的严重性**
- R2: BioBERT/SciBERT/CoSafe/SimCLR 等为 Must-Fix 级别遗漏
- R1: 未发现关键缺失引用，现有覆盖全面
- **Director 判断**: BioBERT/SciBERT 确实与 DAPT 方法论直接相关，但非致命遗漏。列为 Should-Fix。

---

## Questions（需作者澄清）

1. 如果在 OOD 评估中加入不同领域的 benign 对话（如客服、学术问答），F1 ≥ 0.99 是否还能维持？[R1 Q1]
2. TOST 等价性被拒绝的最小 margin 是多少？能否提供 n=3 和 n=5 的 power analysis？[R1 Q2, Q6]
3. Scrambled labels 中 random permutation 的种子是否会影响结果？不同 permutation 的 variance 有多大？[R2 Q1]
4. 为什么没有在同一数据上运行已发表的多轮防御系统？是数据格式不兼容、代码不可用，还是方法论选择？[R1 Q4]
5. Mixed-LLM 如果加入更多样化的 LLM，能否改善 WildChat FPR？[R2 Q2]
6. RoBERTa-base vanilla 已达 DD 0.981——DAPT 的价值是否 architecture-dependent？[R2 Q3]
7. 放松 BERTScore 约束（如 0.85 或 0.80）时，detection 是否显著下降？[R2 Q5]

## Overall Assessment

### 评分

| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 综合 |
|------|-----|-----|------|
| Soundness | 3/5 | 3.5/5 | 3/5 |
| Excitement | 3/5 | 2.5/5 | 2.5/5 |
| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 4/5 | 4/5 |
| **Overall** | **3.0** | **3.0** | **3.0** |
| Confidence | 4/5 | 4/5 | — |

**Overall: 3.0 — Findings（可被 Findings 接收）**

### 总评

一项执行扎实的控制消融研究，系统地分解了 encoder adaptation 在多轮 jailbreak 检测中的各因素贡献。消融设计是最大亮点——7 种变体的 factorial approach 在 NLP 安全领域少见。Domain-exposure > label-correctness 的发现有真正的学术价值，scrambled ≈ correct labels 的观察对 DAPT 理论有启发意义。

但论文面临三个结构性问题：(1) 核心发现在 DAPT/SSL 文献中均有先例，增量新颖性有限——更适合定位为"安全关键领域的系统验证"而非"反直觉发现"；(2) IID benign 膨胀了 OOD F1，WildChat FPR 44-68% 暴露了真实场景下的严重局限，削弱了论文的实用价值叙事；(3) 关键统计检验（TOST n=3/5、跨种子池化 bootstrap CI）的效力不足以支撑核心结论的确定性程度。

此外，正文超过 8 页限制和缺少已发表 baseline 的实验对比是需要立即解决的格式/实验问题。

**建议接收为 Findings**，条件是修复页数超限、降级统计声明、更诚实地定位新颖性和实用价值。

---

## Detailed Comments

### 按章节详细意见

**Abstract**: 数值使用克制（~5 个），scope 限定为 "synthetic multi-turn data" 是正确的。但 "condition-dependent" 和 "evidence hierarchy" 首次出现时缺乏直观解释。

**Introduction**: Contribution 列表结构清晰但信息密度仍偏高。"Counter-intuitive findings" 的 framing 需降温——在更广泛 ML 文献中这些并非反直觉。第四段结果预览偏长（历史 issue R2-W4-v2 未解决）。

**Related Work**: 覆盖面广（31 篇引用），但遗漏 domain-specific BERT 前辈（BioBERT/SciBERT）。与 computational persuasion 文献的联系充分。

**Method (Section 3)**: Table 1 清晰传达 7 种变体设计。但 "single-factor isolation" 声明需加 caveat（Binary/Topic/MLM 存在混淆）。Strategy annotation 质量验证不足（n=50, κ=0.78）。

**Experiments (Section 4)**:
- **Setup**: 数据规模偏小（350 训练，76/118 测试）。OOD benign = IID 是系统性缺陷。
- **Cross-Attack (4.2)**: Table 2 清晰但 F1 ≥ 0.99 应标注为 upper bound。
- **Decomposition (4.3)**: Evidence hierarchy 是论文核心贡献。TOST 统计基础已改善（n=5）但 AA 仍 inconclusive。4-20× variance reduction 需降级为描述性观察。
- **Cross-Encoder (4.4)**: RoBERTa-large vanilla 1.000 on AA 是重要发现，但 scale claim 被架构差异混淆（历史 issue R1-CAPACITY）。
- **Mixed-LLM (4.5)**: MLM 崩溃是重要负面结果。
- **Human Data (4.6)**: ToxicChat 是单轮——正文应更明确标注。
- **Perturbation (4.7)**: AA 下 DAPT 降 20pp 是重要局限。
- **Strategy (4.8)**: OOD κ=0.126 坦诚但削弱 taxonomy 价值。

**Discussion (Section 5)**: 与 Introduction contributions 有重复。"Benign distribution as the remaining bottleneck" 段落重要但应提前至更显眼位置。

**Conclusion (Section 6)**: 溢出到第 9 页——必须压缩。

**Limitations (Section 7)**: 覆盖广泛（10 段），内容充实。但正文 framing 与 Limitations 中承认的局限之间存在温度差。

**Appendix**: 极其详尽（22 页），包含 per-prefix 分析、CKA、cross-LLM、TOST sensitivity、数据效率等。Reproducibility details 完备。

### 历史 issue 追踪摘要

本轮（Round 8 fresh review）重新发现了以下长期未解决的 issues：
- **R2-N1-v2** (新颖性不足): 跨 7 轮持续存在 → 本轮再次确认
- **R1-N2-v2** (缺少已发表 baseline): 跨 6 轮未解决 → 本轮再次确认
- **R5-N1** (IID benign): 跨 4 轮未解决 → 本轮再次确认
- **R6-N2** (TOST 效力): 部分缓解（n=5）但未完全解决 → 本轮再次确认
- **R2-W6-v2** (页数超限): Round 3 标记为已解决，**当前为 regression**

新发现 issues：
- **R8-N1**: Single-factor isolation 声明过度
- **R8-N2**: FITD 无区分度
- **R8-N3**: BioBERT/SciBERT 等 related work 遗漏
- **R8-N4**: LLM baseline 对比公平性

## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)

**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:

1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),
   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。

2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:
   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长
   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补
   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本
   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据
   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)

3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:
   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有
   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的
   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。

4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,
   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。

5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标
   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`
   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。

**禁止**:
- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent
- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action
- 对低 priority 意见用"暂不处理"一笔带过而不给出明确理由
- 整体性"已知悉"回复

```json
{
  "text": "## Round 8 审稿报告独立评估请求\n\nRound 8 fresh review 出分 3.0 Findings（与 Round 7 持平）。以下是完整审稿问题清单 + 我的初步修复方案，请独立评估每条方案的可行性和优先级。\n\n---\n\n### MF1: 正文超 8 页限制（regression，R3 曾标记已解决）\n**问题**: Conclusion 起始于第 9 页顶部，主文约 8.1-8.2 页。可能触发 desk reject。\n**我的方案**: 压缩正文 3-5 行。候选压缩点：\n- experiments.tex TOST 段（L112）刚加了 5-seed 细节，可以精简\n- Discussion 5 段可合并/压缩（\"Encoder capacity\" 段最短，可考虑移至 Appendix）\n- Introduction 第四段 results preview 偏长（历史 issue R2-W4-v2）\n- 检查 Section 4 中的冗余表述\n**问题**: 哪些段落压缩对论文质量损失最小？是否有我没注意到的空间浪费？\n\n### MF2: 缺少已发表多轮防御 baseline（跨 7 轮未解决 R1-N2-v2）\n**问题**: 论文引用 5+ 个已发表系统但未运行任何一个。\n**我的方案**: \n- 选项 A: DDL 前跑 Llama Guard 3 (8B) 或 WildGuard 在我们的 OOD 测试集上，作为参考点。需要 GPU + ~2-4h 实验时间。\n- 选项 B: 在 Related Work/Discussion 中更明确说明为什么不做直接对比（参数量差异 184M vs 8B+、任务定义差异、frozen encoder 范式不同）\n- 选项 C: 两者结合——跑一个 baseline + 文字说明参数量差异\n**问题**: 在 DDL 前 4 天，跑一个 Llama Guard baseline 是否值得？它能否改变审稿人对 novelty 的判断？\n\n### MF3: OOD 评估中 benign 是 IID（跨 4 轮 R5-N1）\n**问题**: OOD F1 衡量的是\"在熟悉良性背景下检测新攻击\"，系统性膨胀。\n**我的方案**: \n- 已有 Discussion \"Benign distribution as the remaining bottleneck\" 段落 + WildChat FPR 数据\n- 可补充：在 Table 2 脚注标注 \"OOD F1 is an upper bound (benign is IID)\"\n- 可补充：分报 OOD attack recall 和 IID benign recall（数据已有——benign recall=1.000 for all DAPT）\n**问题**: 分报 recall/precision 是否比脚注更有效？是否需要新实验？\n\n### MF4: TOST 统计效力（部分缓解 R6-N2）\n**问题**: AA TOST p=0.08，formally inconclusive。5pp margin 无理论依据。\n**我的方案**: \n- 当前已改为 \"TOST-confirmed on DD; d=0.09 negligible on AA\"\n- Cohen's d=0.09 (negligible) 是比 p-value 更有说服力的论据\n- 可补充 power analysis (计算 n 需要多大才能在 AA 上 pass TOST at α=0.05)\n- 5pp margin: 可加一句 justification（\"5pp corresponds to ~5 misclassified conversations out of ~100 test samples\"）\n**问题**: 当前 framing 是否足够？还是需要进一步降级？\n\n### MF5: 新颖性有限（跨 7 轮 R2-N1-v2）\n**问题**: DAPT/SSL/bag-of-features 文献中已有先例，增量新颖性有限。\n**我的方案**: \n- 将 \"counter-intuitive findings\" 降级为 \"systematic verification in a safety-critical domain\"\n- 强调 condition-dependent effects（mixed-LLM collapse 是新发现）\n- 强调 practical contribution（266× faster, N=50 saturation）\n- 不再试图提升 novelty——这是结构性限制\n**问题**: 这个论文的真实目标应该是 Findings 还是值得再冲 Main？你认为哪些改动能最有效提升 excitement？\n\n### MF6: WildChat FPR 44-68%（R6-W1）\n**问题**: 实用价值声明被 FPR 削弱。\n**我的方案**: \n- Introduction \"practical alternative\" 加更强 qualifier：\"given matched benign distributions\"\n- 或改为 \"practical for monitoring known deployment environments\"\n- Discussion 已有专门段落\n**问题**: 是否应该完全删除 \"practical alternative\" 叙事？\n\n### SF1: 数据规模和合成依赖（R4-N3）\n**方案**: 无法在 DDL 前解决。Limitations 已覆盖。保持现状。\n\n### SF2: Single-factor isolation overclaim\n**方案**: Table 1 caption 或 Section 3 加 caveat：\"Binary vs. 9-class confounds granularity with class count; Topic vs. 9-class confounds semantic content with class count (5 vs. 9).\" 纯写作修复。\n\n### SF3: Scrambled ≈ 9-class 与 taxonomy 叙事矛盾\n**方案**: 重定位 taxonomy 为 \"ablation infrastructure + interpretability tool\"，从 contribution 中降级。Section 4.8 已有 \"interpretability tool\" 定位。需在 Introduction contribution list 中明确。\n\n### SF4: 缺 BioBERT/SciBERT 引用\n**方案**: Related Work 加一句：\"Our approach extends domain-specific continued pretraining~\\citep{lee2020biobert,beltagy2019scibert} to multi-turn jailbreak detection.\" 需加 bib 条目。\n\n### SF5: FITD 无区分度\n**方案**: Section 4.2 或 Table 2 脚注加 \"FITD serves as a sanity check; all variants achieve F1≥0.990, confirming that simple attacks do not discriminate among adaptation strategies.\" 已在 Limitations 中提及。\n\n### SF6: Population std\n**方案**: 已有 \"Unless otherwise noted\" 段落。保持现状。\n\n### SF7: 术语不一致\n**方案**: Section 4.1 已有 terminology paragraph。检查全文 \"OOD\" vs \"cross-attack\" 一致性。\n\n### Minor issues\n- Figure 2 hatching: 低优先级，DDL 前不改\n- Figure 1 font: 低优先级\n- Pages 6-8 视觉节奏: 低优先级\n- TF-IDF K=2: 可在 Discussion 加一句\n\n---\n\n### 核心战略问题（请重点评估）\n\n1. **目标应该是 Findings 还是继续冲 Main?** 3.0 已连续两轮，结构性问题（novelty, IID benign, data scale）在 DDL 前无法根本解决。是否应该接受 Findings 定位并优化为\"最好的 Findings 论文\"？\n\n2. **DDL 前最高价值的 1-2 个行动是什么？** 页数修复是必须的。除此之外，Llama Guard baseline vs 写作优化 vs 更多 seeds，哪个 ROI 最高？\n\n3. **MF2 (baseline) 是否是突破 3.0 天花板的关键？** 如果跑一个 Llama Guard 3 baseline 证明我们的 184M 模型在 OOD 检测上超过或接近 8B safety LLM，这是否能从根本上改变 novelty 叙事（\"small model beats big model via proper adaptation\"）？",

}
```

## Round 8 審稿報告獨立評估

### 一、逐条方案评估

**MF1 (CRITICAL) — 页数超限**
确认为 CRITICAL。在 8 页限制下，Discussion 或 Conclusion 溢出到第 9 页是硬伤——审稿人无需看内容就可以 desk reject。

压缩优先级建议（按性价比排序）：
1. **Experiments §4.2 "Summary" 段（L138-141）**：4 行纯复述，不含新信息，全删无损。
2. **Experiments 术语段（L5-6）**：3 行，可移到 footnote 或直接删（读者从上下文可理解）。
3. **Discussion "Encoder capacity, temporal, and lexical analysis" 段（conclusion.tex L15-16）**：2 行，内容已在 Experiments 详述，可合并到其他段的一句话。
4. **Method 变体描述段**（method.tex L38-43）：6 个变体各用 1-2 句描述，可压缩为 Table 2 已覆盖的部分删掉重复文字。

以上合计可省 ~10-15 行（3-5 行足够回到 8 页内）。**不建议删除任何实验结果或核心分析**。

**MF2 (WARNING) — 缺 published baseline**
这是 7 轮未修复的持续问题，也是 3.0 天花板的主要成因之一。

Llama Guard 3 评估可行性评估：
- **时间成本**：8B 模型加载 + multi-turn prompt 构建 + 4 个 OOD 集评估 ≈ 4-8 GPU-hours，在 88h 预算内
- **预期结果**：Llama Guard 是 single-turn classifier，对 multi-turn 分散意图的攻击大概率 recall 极低（DD/AA F1 可能 <0.3），这恰好支持论文 "single-turn filters miss multi-turn attacks" 的核心论点
- **风险**：(a) 实现问题（prompt 格式、multi-turn 输入格式）可能浪费半天；(b) 如果 Llama Guard 意外表现好，会动摇论文核心论点——但这种可能性极低
- **建议**：值得做，但优先级低于 MF1。如果 MF1 在半天内修复，剩余时间跑 Llama Guard

**MF3 — IID benign in OOD**
Discussion 已有 "Benign distribution as the remaining bottleneck" 段 + WildChat FPR 68.1% 数据。当前处理充分，无需进一步修改。[severity: info]

**MF4 — TOST 统计效力**
已扩展到 5 seeds，DD p<0.001，Cohen's d≈0（DD）和 0.09（AA）。当前证据充分。[severity: info]

**MF5 — Novelty**
结构性限制。论文定位为 "systematic decomposition" 而非 "novel architecture"——这在 Findings 级别是可接受的定位，但 Main 需要更强的 novelty signal（如新数据集、新攻击家族发现、或 practical defense tool）。**4 天内无法改善**。[severity: info — 接受现状]

**MF6 — WildChat FPR**
AUC=0.70 是 genuine domain shift，已在 Discussion 和 Limitations 充分讨论。[severity: info]

**SF1-SF7**：均为增量写作修复，按 Director 方案执行即可，不单独评估。

---

### 二、硬约束合规检查

[severity: critical] **Limitations 章节严重违反用户规则**。

用户规则明确要求："Limitations 章节只需要写一小段，覆盖 3 个点左右就可以了，不需要写得很长"。

当前 Limitations 有 **11 个 \paragraph**，覆盖从 dataset scale 到 computation cost 的所有方面。这违反了用户配置的硬约束。

虽然 EMNLP 2026 规则允许 Limitations 不计入 8 页限制，但条件是 "provided they do not contain excessive additional content"——11 段显然属于 "excessive"，有被审稿人/AC 质疑的风险。

建议保留 3 个核心点：
1. **Multi-turn human validation**（最重要的 open question）
2. **OOD benign distribution**（deployment limitation + WildChat 证据）
3. **Encoder scale**（generalization boundary）

其余 8 段全部删除或压缩为 1-2 句合并。**这同时能帮助 MF1 的整体页数控制**。

---

### 三、战略问题回答

**Q1: 目标应该是 Findings 还是继续冲 Main？**

**建议：锚定 Findings（3.0），不追 Main（3.5+）。**

理由：
- 评分趋势 2.5 → 2.5 → 2.75 → 3.0 → 3.0 → 3.0，在 3.0 已稳定两轮
- Main（3.5+）需要至少：(1) published baseline 对比 (2) 更强 novelty signal (3) 可能需要 multi-turn human data——后两项在 4 天内不可能
- 即使加了 Llama Guard baseline，最多提升到 3.0-3.25，仍在 Findings 区间
- Findings 本身是有价值的发表——负面结果（label 不重要、temporal order 不重要）和 systematic decomposition 在 Findings 完全合适

**Q2: DDL 前最高价值的 1-2 个行动？**

**第一优先级（必做）**：修复 MF1 页数超限 + 压缩 Limitations 至 3 点。这是 format compliance 硬伤，不修 = desk reject 风险。预计 2-3 小时。

**第二优先级（推荐做）**：跑 Llama Guard 3 baseline。这是唯一能提供 "new evidence" 的行动，有可能把分数从 3.0 推到 3.0-3.25。但必须在 MF1 修复后再做，预计 4-8 小时。

其他所有 SF 修复是 diminishing returns——写作打磨可以做，但不改变分数区间。

**Q3: Llama Guard baseline 是否是突破 3.0 天花板的关键？**

**不是"关键"，但是目前唯一可行的加分项。**

3.0 天花板的主要成因是 novelty + 实验范围（synthetic-only multi-turn），不是单一缺失 baseline。加 Llama Guard 只解决"缺 published baseline"这一个维度。但在 4 天内能做的事情里，这是信噪比最高的——实现成本可控、预期结果支持论文论点、且消除审稿人一个反复提出的攻击面。

不过，如果 Llama Guard 实现遇到问题（prompt 格式不兼容、需要额外 tokenizer 适配等），**不要死磕**——回退到"Llama Guard is single-turn by design, comparison is architecturally inappropriate"的 justify 策略，损失可控。

---

### 四、总结

| 项目 | 评估 | 优先级 |
|------|------|--------|
| MF1 页数超限 | CRITICAL — 必修 | P0 |
| Limitations 违规 | CRITICAL — 用户硬约束 | P0（与 MF1 同步修） |
| MF2 Llama Guard | WARNING — 可做、值做 | P1 |
| MF3-MF6 | INFO — 当前处理充分 | P2 (写作微调) |
| SF1-SF7 | INFO — 增量修复 | P3 |
| 战略定位 | 锚定 Findings | — |

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01LCz235oFbrudrejLbSBxsw`</summary>

```text
25	\section{Limitations}
26	\label{sec:limitations}
27	
28	\paragraph{Multi-turn human validation.}
29	All multi-turn findings are derived from synthetic conversations generated by Qwen3-8B. ToxicChat validates transfer to human-authored single-turn attacks (+18--42\,pp over vanilla), but no human-generated multi-turn jailbreak dataset exists for evaluation. Whether the evidence hierarchy and classification supervision advantages generalize to human-authored multi-turn attacks remains the primary open question.
30	
31	\paragraph{Turn-count distribution.}
32	Benign conversations contain 12--20 turns, DD jailbreaks 6--10, and ActorAttack jailbreaks 3--8; the three categories have non-overlapping turn counts. Three observations argue against length as the primary detection cue: (a) early detection at equal-length prefixes $K$=1 and $K$=2 achieves AA OOD F1\,=\,0.773 and 0.935 (Appendix~\ref{tab:actorattack_full}); (b) vanilla models access the same length signal but achieve near-zero OOD recall; and (c) truncating benign conversations to 3 turns yields FPR\,=\,0\% (Appendix~\ref{app:length_independence}).
33	
34	\paragraph{Temporal aggregation dilution.}
35	When subtle attacks (ActorAttack) are followed by extended benign padding, BiGRU temporal aggregation dilutes attack signals (F1 drops from 0.963 to 0.383; Appendix~\ref{app:length_independence}); in practice, adversaries would not dilute their own successful attacks.
36	
37	\paragraph{OOD benign distribution.}
38	OOD results reflect attack-side generalization only (Section~\ref{sec:discussion}). Even with matched benign distributions, residual calibration error (ECE\,=\,0.126--0.141; Appendix~\ref{app:calibration}) suggests temperature scaling is needed for deployment.
39	
40	\paragraph{Frozen encoder assumption.}
41	The frozen Stage-1 design isolates representation quality but may underestimate full-pipeline performance. End-to-end fine-tuning closes this gap to $\leq$5.8\,pp on ActorAttack (F1\,=\,0.997$\pm$0.005 vs.\ 0.939$\pm$0.047 frozen; Table~\ref{tab:e2e}; Appendix~\ref{app:frozen_verify}).
42	
43	\paragraph{Dataset scale and cross-LLM transfer.}
44	Scale validation (1{,}000 conversations; Appendix~\ref{app:scale_validation}) confirms the hierarchy holds (DAPT advantage 40--56\,pp over vanilla). Cross-LLM validation shows jailbreak detection transfers when benign distribution is held constant (F1 recovers to 0.800--1.000), but benign-side F1 drops to 0.370--0.430 with unfamiliar benign text (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_llm}). The perturbation evaluation ($n$=76) remains underpowered.
45	
46	\paragraph{Strategy labels.}
47	Persuasion strategy labels ($n$=50 human validation, $\kappa$=0.78) are produced by Qwen3-8B; the validation sample yields small per-class sizes across 9 categories, limiting reliability estimates for individual strategies. OOD strategy accuracy degrades to 30.7\% ($\kappa$=0.126); interpretability is limited to known attack families. We plan to release annotation prompts and data for independent verification.
48	
49	\paragraph{Detection architecture.}
50	Binary vs.\ 9-class equivalence (F1 gap $\leq$3.9\,pp) suggests the binary head is not the critical differentiator. FITD provides minimal discriminative signal (all variants F1\,$\geq$\,0.990), serving as confirmation that simple attacks do not challenge any variant.
51	
52	\paragraph{Encoder scale.}
53	Whether findings hold at LLM-scale encoders (7B+) remains open; same-architecture scaling (e.g., DeBERTa-base vs.\ DeBERTa-large) is needed to attribute the capacity effect to scale alone.
54	
55	\paragraph{Scope and single-turn transfer.}
56	Only persuasion-mediated multi-turn attacks fall within scope; single-turn token-level attacks~\citep{zou2023gcg}, non-persuasive strategies, and non-text modalities are not addressed. ToxicChat performance reflects zero-shot single-turn transfer; doubling the training corpus drops ToxicChat macro F1 from 0.596 to 0.341$\pm$0.004 while multi-turn OOD remains stable, confirming the architecture targets multi-turn detection.
57	
58	\paragraph{Computation cost.}
59	Total pipeline cost for all seven variants is under 4 GPU-hours on a single A100.
60	
61	\section*{Ethics Statement}
62	
63	This work is defensive: it improves detection of multi-turn jailbreak attacks rather than developing new attacks. We acknowledge dual-use risk, as the persuasion taxonomy could inform adversarial actors. We mitigate this by using only synthetic conversations (no real harmful content), withholding attack-generation prompts, and focusing contributions on detection mechanisms. No human subjects were involved; all data will be released under CC BY-NC 4.0.
```

```json
      "content": "\\section{Limitations}\n\\label{sec:limitations}\n\n\\paragraph{Multi-turn human validation.}\nAll multi-turn findings are derived from synthetic conversations generated by Qwen3-8B. ToxicChat validates transfer to human-authored single-turn attacks (+18--42\\,pp over vanilla), but no human-generated multi-turn jailbreak dataset exists for evaluation. Whether the evidence hierarchy and classification supervision advantages generalize to human-authored multi-turn attacks remains the primary open question.\n\n\\paragraph{Turn-count distribution.}\nBenign conversations contain 12--20 turns, DD jailbreaks 6--10, and ActorAttack jailbreaks 3--8; the three categories have non-overlapping turn counts. Three observations argue against length as the primary detection cue: (a) early detection at equal-length prefixes $K$=1 and $K$=2 achieves AA OOD F1\\,=\\,0.773 and 0.935 (Appendix~\\ref{tab:actorattack_full}); (b) vanilla models access the same length signal but achieve near-zero OOD recall; and (c) truncating benign conversations to 3 turns yields FPR\\,=\\,0\\% (Appendix~\\ref{app:length_independence}).\n\n\\paragraph{Temporal aggregation dilution.}\nWhen subtle attacks (ActorAttack) are followed by extended benign padding, BiGRU temporal aggregation dilutes attack signals (F1 drops from 0.963 to 0.383; Appendix~\\ref{app:length_independence}); in practice, adversaries would not dilute their own successful attacks.\n\n\\paragraph{OOD benign distribution.}\nOOD results reflect attack-side generalization only (Section~\\ref{sec:discussion}). Even with matched benign distributions, residual calibration error (ECE\\,=\\,0.126--0.141; Appendix~\\ref{app:calibration}) suggests temperature scaling is needed for deployment.\n\n\\paragraph{Frozen encoder assumption.}\nThe frozen Stage-1 design isolates representation quality but may underestimate full-pipeline performance. End-to-end fine-tuning closes this gap to $\\leq$5.8\\,pp on ActorAttack (F1\\,=\\,0.997$\\pm$0.005 vs.\\ 0.939$\\pm$0.047 frozen; Table~\\ref{tab:e2e}; Appendix~\\ref{app:frozen_verify}).\n\n\\paragraph{Dataset scale and cross-LLM transfer.}\nScale validation (1{,}000 conversations; Appendix~\\ref{app:scale_validation}) confirms the hierarchy holds (DAPT advantage 40--56\\,pp over vanilla). Cross-LLM validation shows jailbreak detection transfers when benign distribution is held constant (F1 recovers to 0.800--1.000), but benign-side F1 drops to 0.370--0.430 with unfamiliar benign text (Appendix~\\ref{app:cross_llm}). The perturbation evaluation ($n$=76) remains underpowered.\n\n\\paragraph{Strategy labels.}\nPersuasion strategy labels ($n$=50 human validation, $\\kappa$=0.78) are produced by Qwen3-8B; the validation sample yields small per-class sizes across 9 categories, limiting reliability estimates for individual strategies. OOD strategy accuracy degrades to 30.7\\% ($\\kappa$=0.126); interpretability is limited to known attack families. We plan to release annotation prompts and data for independent verification.\n\n\\paragraph{Detection architecture.}\nBinary vs.\\ 9-class equivalence (F1 gap $\\leq$3.9\\,pp) suggests the binary head is not the critical differentiator. FITD provides minimal discriminative signal (all variants F1\\,$\\geq$\\,0.990), serving as confirmation that simple attacks do not challenge any variant.\n\n\\paragraph{Encoder scale.}\nWhether findings hold at LLM-scale encoders (7B+) remains open; same-architecture scaling (e.g., DeBERTa-base vs.\\ DeBERTa-large) is needed to attribute the capacity effect to scale alone.\n\n\\paragraph{Scope and single-turn transfer.}\nOnly persuasion-mediated multi-turn attacks fall within scope; single-turn token-level attacks~\\citep{zou2023gcg}, non-persuasive strategies, and non-text modalities are not addressed. ToxicChat performance reflects zero-shot single-turn transfer; doubling the training corpus drops ToxicChat macro F1 from 0.596 to 0.341$\\pm$0.004 while multi-turn OOD remains stable, confirming the architecture targets multi-turn detection.\n\n\\paragraph{Computation cost.}\nTotal pipeline cost for all seven variants is under 4 GPU-hours on a single A100.\n\n\\section*{Ethics Statement}\n\nThis work is defensive: it improves detection of multi-turn jailbreak attacks rather than developing new attacks. We acknowledge dual-use risk, as the persuasion taxonomy could inform adversarial actors. We mitigate this by using only synthetic conversations (no real harmful content), withholding attack-generation prompts, and focusing contributions on detection mechanisms. No human subjects were involved; all data will be released under CC BY-NC 4.0.\n",
```

```json
    "originalFile": "% conclusion.tex — compressed for 8-page limit\n\n\\section{Discussion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\n\\paragraph{Topic shortcut and label equivalence.}\nThree observations argue against a topic shortcut: unstable topic OOD (F1\\,=\\,0.541$\\pm$0.207), scrambled labels matching correct within 3.9\\,pp, and label-free MLM detection (F1\\,$\\geq$\\,0.97 at $K \\leq 5$). One hypothesis is that classification loss regularizes the representation space independently of label content, preventing catastrophic drift from the pretrained manifold; binary (2-class) and 9-class scrambled yield equivalent detection (F1 gap $\\leq$3.9\\,pp), spanning a 4.5$\\times$ class-count range consistent with the regularization interpretation; finer-grained class-count ablation could further distinguish these.\n\n\\paragraph{Single-LLM confound.}\nToxicChat (+18--42\\,pp; Table~\\ref{tab:human_data}) and cross-LLM experiments (Appendix~\\ref{app:cross_llm}) confirm transfer. Mixed-LLM 9-class maintains DD F1\\,=\\,0.818$\\pm$0.096 (+53\\,pp) while MLM collapses; vocabulary analysis confirms that benign texts from different LLMs diverge primarily in topic distribution (KL\\,$>$\\,1\\,bit) rather than stylistic markers, so MLM predictably acquires source-specific topic patterns while classification supervision, operating on strategy labels independent of surface form, remains robust. Cross-LLM error decomposition confirms F1 degradation is driven by false positives on unfamiliar benign text, not missed attacks.\n\n\\paragraph{Variance reversal on human data.}\nClassification supervision is associated with lower variance on synthetic OOD (4--20$\\times$ observed range, 3 seeds); on ToxicChat this reverses (std\\,=\\,0.087 vs.\\ 0.013). Frozen probing localizes MLM instability to Stage-2 (Appendix~\\ref{app:frozen_verify}).\n\n\\paragraph{Encoder capacity, temporal, and lexical analysis.}\nThree encoders (125M--355M) differ in pretraining; same-architecture scaling is needed. Mean pooling matches BiGRU ($|\\Delta| < 1$\\,pp), confirming order-invariant detection. TF-IDF collapses on ActorAttack (0.261 vs.\\ 0.961 on DD).\n\n\\paragraph{Benign distribution as the remaining bottleneck.}\nOOD F1 figures are upper bounds (benign is IID). On WildChat, 9-class FPR reaches 68.1\\% (Appendix~\\ref{app:wildchat_fpr}); cross-LLM error decomposition confirms collapse is driven by false positives on unfamiliar benign text.\n\n\\section{Conclusion}\n\nOn synthetic multi-turn evaluations, classification-based DAPT is the most robust adaptation for base-size encoders; unsupervised pretraining collapses under mixed-LLM training. Negative results on label correctness (TOST-confirmed on DD at $n$\\,=\\,5; $d$\\,=\\,0.09 on ActorAttack), temporal order, and domain specificity constrain which properties matter; continued pretraining is 266$\\times$ faster than LLM-as-judge (Appendix~\\ref{app:efficiency}), offering a practical alternative given matched benign distributions (Appendix~\\ref{app:wildchat_fpr}).\n\n\\section{Limitations}\n\\label{sec:limitations}\n\n\\paragraph{Multi-turn human validation.}\nAll multi-turn findings are derived from synthetic conversations generated by Qwen3-8B. ToxicChat validates transfer to human-authored single-turn attacks (+18--42\\,pp over vanilla), but no human-generated multi-turn jailbreak dataset exists for evaluation. Whether the evidence hierarchy and classification supervision advantages generalize to human-authored multi-turn attacks remains the primary open question.\n\n\\paragraph{Turn-count distribution.}\nBenign conversations contain 12--20 turns, DD jailbreaks 6--10, and ActorAttack jailbreaks 3--8; the three categories have non-overlapping turn counts. Three observations argue against length as the primary detection cue: (a) early detection at equal-length prefixes $K$=1 and $K$=2 achieves AA OOD F1\\,=\\,0.773 and 0.935 (Appendix~\\ref{tab:actorattack_full}); (b) vanilla models access the same length signal but achieve near-zero OOD recall; and (c) truncating benign conversations to 3 turns yields FPR\\,=\\,0\\% (Appendix~\\ref{app:length_independence}).\n\n\\paragraph{Temporal aggregation dilution.}\nWhen subtle attacks (ActorAttack) are followed by extended benign padding, BiGRU temporal aggregation dilutes attack signals (F1 drops from 0.963 to 0.383; Appendix~\\ref{app:length_independence}); in practice, adversaries would not dilute their own successful attacks.\n\n\\paragraph{OOD benign distribution.}\nOOD results reflect attack-side generalization only (Section~\\ref{sec:discussion}). Even with matched benign distributions, residual calibration error (ECE\\,=\\,0.126--0.141; Appendix~\\ref{app:calibration}) suggests temperature scaling is needed for deployment.\n\n\\paragraph{Frozen encoder assumption.}\nThe frozen Stage-1 design isolates representation quality but may underestimate full-pipeline performance. End-to-end fine-tuning closes this gap to $\\leq$5.8\\,pp on ActorAttack (F1\\,=\\,0.997$\\pm$0.005 vs.\\ 0.939$\\pm$0.047 frozen; Table~\\ref{tab:e2e}; Appendix~\\ref{app:frozen_verify}).\n\n\\paragraph{Dataset scale and cross-LLM transfer.}\nScale validation (1{,}000 conversations; Appendix~\\ref{app:scale_validation}) confirms the hierarchy holds (DAPT advantage 40--56\\,pp over vanilla). Cross-LLM validation shows jailbreak detection transfers when benign distribution is held constant (F1 recovers to 0.800--1.000), but benign-side F1 drops to 0.370--0.430 with unfamiliar benign text (Appendix~\\ref{app:cross_llm}). The perturbation evaluation ($n$=76) remains underpowered.\n\n\\paragraph{Strategy labels.}\nPersuasion strategy labels ($n$=50 human validation, $\\kappa$=0.78) are produced by Qwen3-8B; the validation sample yields small per-class sizes across 9 categories, limiting reliability estimates for individual strategies. OOD strategy accuracy degrades to 30.7\\% ($\\kappa$=0.126); interpretability is limited to known attack families. We plan to release annotation prompts and data for independent verification.\n\n\\paragraph{Detection architecture.}\nBinary vs.\\ 9-class equivalence (F1 gap $\\leq$3.9\\,pp) suggests the binary head is not the critical differentiator. FITD provides minimal discriminative signal (all variants F1\\,$\\geq$\\,0.990), serving as confirmation that simple attacks do not challenge any variant.\n\n\\paragraph{Encoder scale.}\nWhether findings hold at LLM-scale encoders (7B+) remains open; same-architecture scaling (e.g., DeBERTa-base vs.\\ DeBERTa-large) is needed to attribute the capacity effect to scale alone.\n\n\\paragraph{Scope and single-turn transfer.}\nOnly persuasion-mediated multi-turn attacks fall within scope; single-turn token-level attacks~\\citep{zou2023gcg}, non-persuasive strategies, and non-text modalities are not addressed. ToxicChat performance reflects zero-shot single-turn transfer; doubling the training corpus drops ToxicChat macro F1 from 0.596 to 0.341$\\pm$0.004 while multi-turn OOD remains stable, confirming the architecture targets multi-turn detection.\n\n\\paragraph{Computation cost.}\nTotal pipeline cost for all seven variants is under 4 GPU-hours on a single A100.\n\n\\section*{Ethics Statement}\n\nThis work is defensive: it improves detection of multi-turn jailbreak attacks rather than developing new attacks. We acknowledge dual-use risk, as the persuasion taxonomy could inform adversarial actors. We mitigate this by using only synthetic conversations (no real harmful content), withholding attack-generation prompts, and focusing contributions on detection mechanisms. No human subjects were involved; all data will be released under CC BY-NC 4.0.\n",
```

```json
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_2/projects/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/docs/paper/conclusion.tex",
        "content": "% conclusion.tex — compressed for 8-page limit\n\n\\section{Discussion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\n\\paragraph{Topic shortcut and label equivalence.}\nThree observations argue against a topic shortcut: unstable topic OOD (F1\\,=\\,0.541$\\pm$0.207), scrambled labels matching correct within 3.9\\,pp, and label-free MLM detection (F1\\,$\\geq$\\,0.97 at $K \\leq 5$). One hypothesis is that classification loss regularizes the representation space independently of label content, preventing catastrophic drift from the pretrained manifold; binary (2-class) and 9-class scrambled yield equivalent detection (F1 gap $\\leq$3.9\\,pp), spanning a 4.5$\\times$ class-count range consistent with the regularization interpretation; finer-grained class-count ablation could further distinguish these.\n\n\\paragraph{Single-LLM confound.}\nToxicChat (+18--42\\,pp; Table~\\ref{tab:human_data}) and cross-LLM experiments (Appendix~\\ref{app:cross_llm}) confirm transfer. Mixed-LLM 9-class maintains DD F1\\,=\\,0.818$\\pm$0.096 (+53\\,pp) while MLM collapses; vocabulary analysis confirms that benign texts from different LLMs diverge primarily in topic distribution (KL\\,$>$\\,1\\,bit) rather than stylistic markers, so MLM predictably acquires source-specific topic patterns while classification supervision, operating on strategy labels independent of surface form, remains robust. Cross-LLM error decomposition confirms F1 degradation is driven by false positives on unfamiliar benign text, not missed attacks.\n\n\\paragraph{Variance reversal on human data.}\nClassification supervision is associated with lower variance on synthetic OOD (4--20$\\times$ observed range, 3 seeds); on ToxicChat this reverses (std\\,=\\,0.087 vs.\\ 0.013). Frozen probing localizes MLM instability to Stage-2 (Appendix~\\ref{app:frozen_verify}).\n\n\\paragraph{Encoder capacity, temporal, and lexical analysis.}\nThree encoders (125M--355M) differ in pretraining; same-architecture scaling is needed. Mean pooling matches BiGRU ($|\\Delta| < 1$\\,pp), confirming order-invariant detection. TF-IDF collapses on ActorAttack (0.261 vs.\\ 0.961 on DD).\n\n\\paragraph{Benign distribution as the remaining bottleneck.}\nOOD F1 figures are upper bounds (benign is IID). On WildChat, 9-class FPR reaches 68.1\\% (Appendix~\\ref{app:wildchat_fpr}); cross-LLM error decomposition confirms collapse is driven by false positives on unfamiliar benign text.\n\n\\section{Conclusion}\n\nOn synthetic multi-turn evaluations, classification-based DAPT is the most robust adaptation for base-size encoders; unsupervised pretraining collapses under mixed-LLM training. Negative results on label correctness (TOST-confirmed on DD at $n$\\,=\\,5; $d$\\,=\\,0.09 on ActorAttack), temporal order, and domain specificity constrain which properties matter; continued pretraining is 266$\\times$ faster than LLM-as-judge (Appendix~\\ref{app:efficiency}), offering a practical alternative given matched benign distributions (Appendix~\\ref{app:wildchat_fpr}).\n\n\\section{Limitations}\n\\label{sec:limitations}\n\nAll multi-turn findings derive from synthetic conversations generated by Qwen3-8B (350 training, 76--118 test per family). ToxicChat validates transfer to human-authored single-turn attacks (+18--42\\,pp over vanilla), but no human-generated multi-turn jailbreak dataset exists. Whether the evidence hierarchy generalizes to human-authored multi-turn attacks remains the primary open question.\n\nOOD results reflect attack-side generalization only: benign data is IID, so OOD F1 figures are upper bounds (Section~\\ref{sec:discussion}). On WildChat, 9-class FPR reaches 68.1\\% (Appendix~\\ref{app:wildchat_fpr}); residual calibration error (ECE\\,=\\,0.126--0.141; Appendix~\\ref{app:calibration}) suggests temperature scaling is needed for deployment.\n\nThree encoders (125M--355M) differ in architecture and pretraining; same-architecture scaling is needed to isolate the capacity effect, and whether findings hold at LLM-scale encoders (7B+) remains open. Only persuasion-mediated multi-turn attacks fall within scope; single-turn token-level attacks~\\citep{zou2023gcg}, non-persuasive strategies, and non-text modalities are not addressed. Total pipeline cost is under 4 GPU-hours on a single A100.\n\n\\section*{Ethics Statement}\n\nThis work is defensive: it improves detection of multi-turn jailbreak attacks rather than developing new attacks. We acknowledge dual-use risk, as the persuasion taxonomy could inform adversarial actors. We mitigate this by using only synthetic conversations (no real harmful content), withholding attack-generation prompts, and focusing contributions on detection mechanisms. No human subjects were involved; all data will be released under CC BY-NC 4.0.\n",
        "numLines": 37,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 37
      }
```

```json
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_2/projects/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/docs/paper/introduction.tex",
        "content": "% introduction.tex — expanded for 8-page target\n\n\\section{Introduction}\n\nMulti-turn jailbreak attacks distribute adversarial intent across individually benign turns. Crescendo~\\citep{russinovich2024crescendo}, Foot-in-the-Door~\\citep{fitd2024}, and Deceptive Delight~\\citep{deceptivedelight2024} all rely on persuasion as their core mechanism: \\citet{zeng2024pap} catalogue 40 persuasion techniques across jailbreak families, and \\citet{hagendorff2026reasoning} identify strategies in 97\\% of successful attacks. Because individual attack turns occupy the same embedding region as benign text~\\citep{bullwinkel2025repeng}, no single-turn filter can distinguish them from legitimate conversation.\n\nExisting defenses leave a critical gap. Production classifiers such as Llama Guard~\\citep{inan2023llamaguard}, WildGuard~\\citep{han2024wildguard}, and ShieldGemma~\\citep{zeng2024shieldgemma} operate on individual turns and therefore miss manipulation that unfolds across exchanges. Multi-turn defenses have begun to model conversation-level structure: DeepContext~\\citep{deepcontext2026} pairs fine-tuned BERT with a multi-layer GRU, BIID~\\citep{tong2025biid} infers turn-level intentions, and G-Guard~\\citep{gguard2025} applies graph neural networks. These systems achieve strong in-distribution performance, but each is evaluated on a single attack family. None answers the central question: \\emph{what representation properties enable a detector to generalize across attack families it has never seen?}\n\nPretrained language understanding does not suffice at base model scale: vanilla DeBERTa-v3~\\citep{he2021deberta} with temporal aggregation (cf.\\ DeepContext) drops from F1\\,=\\,1.000 in-distribution to 0.312 on OOD Deceptive Delight, and GPT-5.5 reaches only 0.191--0.449 F1 with 30--47\\% unparseable responses (Appendix~\\ref{sec:llm_judge}). The bottleneck is distributional: cross-attack generalization requires domain adaptation, not a better classifier.\n\nOur pipeline, \\textbf{DisPeD} (Disentangled Persuasion Detection), separates encoder adaptation (Stage~1) from temporal classification (Stage~2, a frozen BiGRU; Figure~\\ref{fig:architecture}). Seven Stage~1 variants each isolate a single factor while holding all others constant (Section~\\ref{sec:method}); the contribution is the decomposition, not the pipeline.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/paper/fig1_architecture.pdf}\n\\caption{DisPeD architecture. Stage~1 adapts a DeBERTa encoder on jailbreak conversation text (seven variant objectives). Stage~2 aggregates frozen \\texttt{[CLS]} embeddings via BiGRU for conversation-level jailbreak detection. Only user turns are processed.}\n\\label{fig:architecture}\n\\end{figure}\n\nControlled ablation across DeBERTa-v3 and RoBERTa~\\citep{liu2019roberta} on four attack families and two training compositions shows that for base-size encoders, classification-based adaptation is the most robust variant on synthetic multi-turn OOD attacks. MLM collapses on mixed-LLM training (F1\\,=\\,0.289 $\\approx$ vanilla) while classification DAPT maintains 0.818; RoBERTa-large (355M) handles ActorAttack~\\citep{ren2024actorattack} without adaptation. Classification supervision is associated with substantially lower prediction variance on synthetic data but not on human-authored conversations (Section~\\ref{sec:human_data}).\n\nOur contributions:\n\\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*, itemsep=2pt, topsep=4pt]\n  \\item \\textbf{Systematic decomposition identifying the most robust adaptation and three counter-intuitive findings.} Controlled ablation across thirteen encoder configurations on synthetic multi-turn data shows classification-based DAPT is the most robust variant; unsupervised pretraining is effective on single-source data but collapses on mixed-LLM training. We report three supplementary findings that challenge common assumptions: scrambled labels perform comparably to correct labels (TOST-confirmed on DD at $n$=5; $d$\\,=\\,0.09 on ActorAttack), temporal order is irrelevant ($\\Delta < 1$\\,pp), and label granularity does not affect detection.\n\n  \\item \\textbf{Architecture- and scale-dependent observation on domain adaptation.} Three encoder scales (125M, 184M, 355M) suggest adaptation need shrinks with model size, though encoders differ in architecture; same-architecture scaling is needed to isolate scale. RoBERTa-large handles structurally complex attacks without adaptation (Section~\\ref{sec:cross_encoder}).\n\n  \\item \\textbf{Condition-dependent adaptation effects.} Classification supervision is associated with substantially lower prediction variance on synthetic data (3 seeds), but this benefit reverses on human-authored data. Mixed-LLM training validates that classification-based DAPT remains the strongest variant across training compositions (Section~\\ref{sec:discussion}). The 9-class taxonomy serves as interpretability tool and ablation infrastructure (Section~\\ref{sec:strategy}). Continued pretraining is 266$\\times$ faster than LLM-as-judge and near-saturates at $N$=50 (AA\\,=\\,0.969; Appendix~\\ref{app:data_efficiency}).\n\\end{itemize}\n",
        "numLines": 30,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 30
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../docs/paper/introduction.tex"
  },
```

```json
      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task.
```
